The results indicate that for models below 7B parameters the main limitation of RAG is context utilization rather than retrieval quality and that deploying RAG at this scale can lead to a net negative trade off under standard evaluation conditions.

</details>


### [111] [One Supervisor, Many Modalities: Adaptive Tool Orchestration for Autonomous Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11545)
*Mayank Saini Arit Kumar Bishwas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个用于自主多模态查询处理的智能AI框架，通过中央监督器动态协调跨文本、图像、音频、视频和文档模态的专用工具，显著提升效率和降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态AI系统通常采用预定义决策树或固定流程处理跨模态查询，缺乏灵活性和智能协调能力，导致效率低下、成本高昂且需要大量人工干预。

Method: 设计中央监督器动态分解用户查询，将子任务委派给模态专用工具（如目标检测、OCR、语音转录），采用自适应路由策略而非预定义决策树。文本查询使用RouteLLM学习路由，非文本路径使用SLM辅助的模态分解。

Result: 在15个任务类别的2,847个查询上评估，相比匹配的层次基线，实现了72%的准确答案获取时间减少、85%的对话返工减少和67%的成本降低，同时保持准确率相当。

Conclusion: 智能集中编排从根本上改善了多模态AI部署的经济性，证明了动态协调和自适应路由在多模态查询处理中的有效性。

Abstract: We present an agentic AI framework for autonomous multimodal query processing that coordinates specialized tools across text, image, audio, video, and document modalities. A central Supervisor dynamically decomposes user queries, delegates subtasks to modality-appropriate tools (e.g., object detection, OCR, speech transcription), and synthesizes results through adaptive routing strategies rather than predetermined decision trees. For text-only queries, the framework uses learned routing via RouteLLM, while non-text paths use SLM-assisted modality decomposition. Evaluated on 2,847 queries across 15 task categories, our framework achieves 72% reduction in time-to-accurate-answer, 85% reduction in conversational rework, and 67% cost reduction compared to the matched hierarchical baseline while maintaining accuracy parity. These results demonstrate that intelligent centralized orchestration fundamentally improves multimodal AI deployment economics.

</details>


### [112] [Where Matters More Than What: Decoding-aligned KV Cache Compression via Position-aware Pseudo Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11564)
*Zhenxu Tian,Yi Su,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DapQ提出了一种基于位置感知伪查询的解码对齐KV缓存压缩框架，通过模拟解码过程来精确评估token重要性，在严格内存限制下实现接近无损的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖输入侧注意力模式在预填充阶段评估token重要性，但无法为未来生成保留关键token，因为这些评估并非来自解码过程。解码阶段查询在推理时不可用，但位置信息比语义内容对构建伪查询更为关键。

Method: 提出DapQ框架，利用位置感知伪查询模拟输出token，建立有效的观察窗口进行重要性评估。该方法与实际生成上下文对齐，实现精确的token淘汰。框架轻量级，通过位置信息而非语义内容构建伪查询。

Result: 在多个基准测试和LLM上的广泛评估表明，DapQ在严格内存约束下实现卓越性能（例如在NIAH基准上，仅使用3% KV缓存预算即可达到99.5%接近无损的性能）。

Conclusion: DapQ通过位置感知伪查询实现解码对齐的KV缓存压缩，有效解决了现有方法无法为未来生成保留关键token的问题，在严格内存限制下保持高性能，为长上下文LLM推理提供了高效的KV缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: The Key-Value (KV) cache is crucial for efficient Large Language Models (LLMs) inference, but excessively long contexts drastically increase KV cache memory footprint. Existing KV cache compression methods typically rely on input-side attention patterns within a prompt observation window to estimate token importance during the prefill stage. They fail to preserve critical tokens for future generation since these assessments are not derived from the decoding process. Intuitively, an effective observation window should mirror the decoding-stage queries to accurately reflect which tokens the generation process will attend to. However, ground-truth decoding queries are inherently unavailable during inference. For constructing pseudo queries to approximate them, we find that positional information plays a more critical role than semantic content. Motivated by this insight, we propose decoding-aligned KV cache compression via position-aware pseudo queries (DapQ), a novel and lightweight eviction framework that leverages position-aware pseudo queries to simulate the output tokens, thereby establishing an effective observation window for importance assessment. It aligns closely with the actual generation context and enables precise token eviction. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks and LLMs demonstrate that DapQ achieves superior performance, particularly under strict memory constraints (e.g., up to nearly lossless performance 99.5% on NIAH with 3% KV cache budgets).

</details>


### [113] [Streaming Translation and Transcription Through Speech-to-Text Causal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11578)
*Roman Koshkin,Jeon Haesung,Lianbo Liu,Hao Shi,Mengjie Zhao,Yusuke Fujita,Yui Sudo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hikari：首个无策略、端到端的同声传译模型，通过概率等待令牌机制编码读/写决策，实现语音到文本的同步翻译和流式转录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同声传译依赖离线机器翻译模型结合人工设计的启发式方法或学习策略，存在复杂性和性能限制。需要更简单、更有效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出概率等待令牌机制，将读/写决策编码到模型中；2) 引入解码器时间膨胀机制，减少自回归开销并平衡训练分布；3) 设计监督微调策略，训练模型从延迟中恢复。

Result: 在英日、英德、英俄翻译任务上，Hikari在低延迟和高延迟场景下均取得新的SOTA BLEU分数，超越了近期基线模型。

Conclusion: Hikari证明了无策略、端到端的同声传译模型的可行性，通过创新的等待机制和时间膨胀技术，显著提升了质量-延迟权衡性能。

Abstract: Simultaneous machine translation (SiMT) has traditionally relied on offline machine translation models coupled with human-engineered heuristics or learned policies. We propose Hikari, a policy-free, fully end-to-end model that performs simultaneous speech-to-text translation and streaming transcription by encoding READ/WRITE decisions into a probabilistic WAIT token mechanism. We also introduce Decoder Time Dilation, a mechanism that reduces autoregressive overhead and ensures a balanced training distribution. Additionally, we present a supervised fine-tuning strategy that trains the model to recover from delays, significantly improving the quality-latency trade-off. Evaluated on English-to-Japanese, German, and Russian, Hikari achieves new state-of-the-art BLEU scores in both low- and high-latency regimes, outperforming recent baselines.

</details>


### [114] [UtilityMax Prompting: A Formal Framework for Multi-Objective Large Language Model Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11583)
*Ofir Marom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UtilityMax Prompting框架，使用形式化数学语言而非自然语言定义LLM任务，通过影响图和效用函数将任务重构为期望效用最大化问题，在多目标电影推荐任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示在多目标任务中存在固有模糊性，导致LLM输出结果不精确。需要更形式化的方法来明确指定任务目标，使LLM能够进行精确的优化推理。

Method: 提出UtilityMax Prompting框架：1) 将任务重构为影响图，其中LLM的答案作为唯一决策变量；2) 在图中条件概率分布上定义效用函数；3) 指导LLM寻找最大化期望效用的答案，强制LLM显式推理每个目标组件。

Result: 在MovieLens 1M数据集上测试三个前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 Pro），多目标电影推荐任务中相比自然语言基线在精确度和NDCG指标上均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 形式化数学语言提示能够约束LLM进行显式目标推理，相比模糊的自然语言提示能更精确地指导LLM输出，在多目标优化任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: The success of a Large Language Model (LLM) task depends heavily on its prompt. Most use-cases specify prompts using natural language, which is inherently ambiguous when multiple objectives must be simultaneously satisfied. In this paper we introduce UtilityMax Prompting, a framework that specifies tasks using formal mathematical language. We reconstruct the task as an influence diagram in which the LLM's answer is the sole decision variable. A utility function is defined over the conditional probability distributions within the diagram, and the LLM is instructed to find the answer that maximises expected utility. This constrains the LLM to reason explicitly about each component of the objective, directing its output toward a precise optimization target rather than a subjective natural language interpretation. We validate our approach on the MovieLens 1M dataset across three frontier models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.4, and Gemini 2.5 Pro), demonstrating consistent improvements in precision and Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) over natural language baselines in a multi-objective movie recommendation task.

</details>


### [115] [Performance Evaluation of Open-Source Large Language Models for Assisting Pathology Report Writing in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11597)
*Masataka Kawai,Singo Sakashita,Shumpei Ishikawa,Shogo Watanabe,Anna Matsuoka,Mikio Sakurai,Yasuto Fujimoto,Yoshiyuki Takahara,Atsushi Ohara,Hirohiko Miyake,Genichiro Ishii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估七个开源大语言模型在日语病理报告写作支持中的表现，涵盖诊断文本生成、信息提取、错字纠正和解释性文本质量评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在支持日语病理报告写作方面的性能尚未得到充分探索，需要评估开源LLMs在临床相关任务中的实际应用潜力

Method: 从三个维度评估七个开源LLMs：1) 按照预定格式生成和提取病理诊断文本；2) 纠正日语病理报告中的拼写错误；3) 由病理学家和临床医生对模型生成的解释性文本进行主观评估

Result: 思维模型和医学专用模型在需要推理的结构化报告任务和错字纠正方面表现优势；解释性输出的偏好在不同评估者之间存在显著差异；LLMs的实用性因任务而异

Conclusion: 开源大语言模型在有限但临床相关的场景中可用于辅助日语病理报告写作，但不同任务需要选择不同类型的模型

Abstract: The performance of large language models (LLMs) for supporting pathology report writing in Japanese remains unexplored. We evaluated seven open-source LLMs from three perspectives: (A) generation and information extraction of pathology diagnosis text following predefined formats, (B) correction of typographical errors in Japanese pathology reports, and (C) subjective evaluation of model-generated explanatory text by pathologists and clinicians. Thinking models and medical-specialized models showed advantages in structured reporting tasks that required reasoning and in typo correction. In contrast, preferences for explanatory outputs varied substantially across raters. Although the utility of LLMs differed by task, our findings suggest that open-source LLMs can be useful for assisting Japanese pathology report writing in limited but clinically relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [QChunker: Learning Question-Aware Text Chunking for Domain RAG via Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11650)
*Jihao Zhao,Daixuan Li,Pengfei Li,Shuaishuai Zu,Biao Qin,Hongyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QChunker通过多智能体辩论框架重构RAG范式，将文本分块建模为文本分割与知识补全的复合任务，确保文本块的逻辑连贯性和完整性，并引入直接评估指标ChunkScore来高效评估分块质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成（RAG）的效果受限于知识库中文本块的语义完整性和信息粒度，现有分块方法存在逻辑不连贯、信息不完整的问题，且评估方法过度依赖下游QA任务，效率低下。

Method: 1. 提出理解-检索-增强新范式；2. 设计多智能体辩论框架，包括问题大纲生成器、文本分割器、完整性审查器和知识补全器；3. 引入ChunkScore直接评估指标；4. 利用文档大纲进行多路径采样生成候选分块，用ChunkScore选择最优解；5. 构建45K高质量数据集并迁移到小语言模型。

Result: 1. 成功构建45K高质量数据集；2. ChunkScore在理论和实验上均证明能直接高效区分文本块质量；3. 在四个异构领域的实验表明，QChunker为RAG提供了更逻辑连贯、信息丰富的文本块，有效解决了原有问题。

Conclusion: QChunker通过重构RAG范式为理解-检索-增强，采用多智能体辩论框架和ChunkScore评估指标，显著提升了文本分块的质量，为RAG系统提供了更高质量的输入，解决了语义完整性和信息粒度约束问题。

Abstract: The effectiveness upper bound of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is fundamentally constrained by the semantic integrity and information granularity of text chunks in its knowledge base. To address these challenges, this paper proposes QChunker, which restructures the RAG paradigm from retrieval-augmentation to understanding-retrieval-augmentation. Firstly, QChunker models the text chunking as a composite task of text segmentation and knowledge completion to ensure the logical coherence and integrity of text chunks. Drawing inspiration from Hal Gregersen's "Questions Are the Answer" theory, we design a multi-agent debate framework comprising four specialized components: a question outline generator, text segmenter, integrity reviewer, and knowledge completer. This framework operates on the principle that questions serve as catalysts for profound insights. Through this pipeline, we successfully construct a high-quality dataset of 45K entries and transfer this capability to small language models. Additionally, to handle long evaluation chains and low efficiency in existing chunking evaluation methods, which overly rely on downstream QA tasks, we introduce a novel direct evaluation metric, ChunkScore. Both theoretical and experimental validations demonstrate that ChunkScore can directly and efficiently discriminate the quality of text chunks. Furthermore, during the text segmentation phase, we utilize document outlines for multi-path sampling to generate multiple candidate chunks and select the optimal solution employing ChunkScore. Extensive experimental results across four heterogeneous domains exhibit that QChunker effectively resolves aforementioned issues by providing RAG with more logically coherent and information-rich text chunks.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced Multimodal LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11665)
*Junjie Wu,Xuan Kan,Zihao He,Shunwen Tan,Bo Pan,Kaitai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MT-RL-Judge：通过多任务强化学习优化MLLM评估器，提升跨任务泛化能力和人类偏好一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估器多为单任务优化，难以泛化到多样化场景，而可靠的评估需要模型具备跨任务泛化能力

Method: 提出MT-RL-Judge框架，利用强化学习在多任务上联合优化MLLM评估器，增强其泛化能力

Result: MT-RL-Judge在多个强基线上表现优异，在判断一致性和人类偏好相关性方面均优于基线，并在分布外任务上展现出鲁棒泛化能力

Conclusion: 多任务强化学习能有效提升MLLM评估器的跨任务泛化性能，为构建更可靠的视觉任务评估系统提供了新途径

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely adopted as MLLM-as-a-Judges due to their strong alignment with human judgment across various visual tasks. However, most existing judge models are optimized for single-task scenarios and struggle to generalize to diverse contexts, which is a critical requirement for reliable evaluation. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for MLLM-as-a-Judge (MT-RL-Judge), a framework that jointly optimizes the judge model across multiple tasks, leveraging the generalization capabilities of RL. Experimental results against several strong baselines demonstrate that MT-RL-Judge outperforms strong baselines in both judgment consistency and correlation with human preferences. Furthermore, our approach exhibits robust generalization on out-of-distribution tasks, further validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [118] [A technology-oriented mapping of the language and translation industry: Analysing stakeholder values and their potential implication for translation pedagogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11667)
*María Isabel Rivas Ginel,Janiça Hackenbuchner,Alina Secară,Ralph Krüger,Caroline Rossi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了自动化语言翻译行业中价值的构建与协商，发现效率导向的技术价值已成为基准期望，而人类价值并未被取代而是重新定位，适应性成为连接人类与技术领域的核心中介价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言翻译行业日益自动化，需要理解价值如何在这种环境中被构建和协商，特别是人类价值、技术价值、效率和适应性在不同专业角色中的表达方式。

Method: 基于LT-LiDER项目收集的29位行业利益相关者的访谈数据，使用Chesterman的翻译伦理框架及相关价值作为分析视角，分析不同专业角色中人类价值、技术价值、效率和适应性的表达方式。

Result: 研究发现：1) 与服务伦理一致的效率导向技术价值已成为自动化生产环境中的基准期望；2) 人类价值并未被取代而是重新定位，主要通过专业知识、监督、责任和情境判断体现；3) 适应性作为连接人类与技术领域的核心中介价值，被构建为专业要求，要求译者持续调整技能、角色和身份以适应工具和组织需求。

Conclusion: 自动化并非取代翻译价值，而是重塑价值，创造了一种相互依存的配置：技术效率使人类交际工作成为可能，人类价值通过技术中介的工作流程重新定位，适应性成为连接两者的关键价值。

Abstract: This paper examines how value is constructed and negotiated in today's increasingly automated language and translation industry. Drawing on interview data from twenty-nine industry stakeholders collected within the LT-LiDER project, the study analyses how human value, technological value, efficiency, and adaptability are articulated across different professional roles. Using Chesterman's framework of translation ethics and associated values as an analytical lens, the paper shows that efficiency-oriented technological values aligned with the ethics of service have become baseline expectations in automated production environments, where speed, scalability, and deliverability dominate evaluation criteria. At the same time, human value is not displaced but repositioned, emerging primarily through expertise, oversight, accountability, and contextual judgment embedded within technology-mediated workflows. A central finding is the prominence of adaptability as a mediating value linking human and technological domains. Adaptability is constructed as a core professional requirement, reflecting expectations that translators continuously adjust their skills, roles, and identities in response to evolving tools and organisational demands. The paper argues that automation reshapes rather than replaces translation value, creating an interdependent configuration in which technological efficiency enables human communicative work.

</details>


### [119] [In the LLM era, Word Sense Induction remains unsolved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11686)
*Anna Mosolova,Marie Candito,Carlos Ramisch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文评估词义归纳方法，发现无监督方法无法超越"每个词元一个聚类"的启发式方法，但通过数据增强和利用Wiktionary能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏标注数据的场景下，词义归纳是词义消歧的重要替代方案，特别是在低资源或特定领域设置中。当前词义归纳评估存在方法论问题，需要更合理的评估框架。

Method: 提出基于SemCor的评估数据集，保持原始语料的多义性和频率分布。评估预训练嵌入和聚类算法，提出基于LLM的词义归纳方法。评估数据增强来源（LLM生成、语料库和词典），以及使用Wiktionary进行数据增强的半监督场景。

Result: 发现无监督方法（包括本文提出的方法）都无法超越"每个词元一个聚类"的启发式方法。结果显示：（1）不同词性的结果和最佳系统可能不同；（2）LLM在执行此任务时存在困难；（3）数据增强有益；（4）利用Wiktionary确实有帮助，在测试集上比之前SOTA系统提升3.3%。

Conclusion: 词义归纳问题尚未解决，需要更好地结合词典和LLM的词汇语义能力。评估框架应更贴近实际语言使用情况，考虑多义性和频率分布。

Abstract: In the absence of sense-annotated data, word sense induction (WSI) is a compelling alternative to word sense disambiguation, particularly in low-resource or domain-specific settings. In this paper, we emphasize methodological problems in current WSI evaluation. We propose an evaluation on a SemCor-derived dataset, respecting the original corpus polysemy and frequency distributions. We assess pre-trained embeddings and clustering algorithms across parts of speech, and propose and evaluate an LLM-based WSI method for English. We evaluate data augmentation sources (LLM-generated, corpus and lexicon), and semi-supervised scenarios using Wiktionary for data augmentation, must-link constraints, number of clusters per lemma.
  We find that no unsupervised method (whether ours or previous) surpasses the strong "one cluster per lemma" heuristic (1cpl). We also show that (i) results and best systems may vary across POS, (ii) LLMs have troubles performing this task, (iii) data augmentation is beneficial and (iv) capitalizing on Wiktionary does help. It surpasses previous SOTA system on our test set by 3.3\%. WSI is not solved, and calls for a better articulation of lexicons and LLMs' lexical semantics capabilities.

</details>


### [120] [SemBench: A Universal Semantic Framework for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11687)
*Mikel Zubillaga,Naiara Perez,Oscar Sainz,German Rigau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemBench：一个使用词典义项定义和句子编码器自动生成评估LLM语义理解能力的合成基准框架，具有可扩展性和语言无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在自然语言处理领域取得了显著进展，但评估其真正的语义理解能力仍然是一个挑战。传统的Word-in-Context等基准测试虽然有效，但创建过程资源密集且通常仅限于高资源语言。

Method: 提出SemBench框架，仅使用词典义项定义和句子编码器自动生成合成基准，无需人工标注的例句。该方法通过对比不同义项定义的语义表示来评估LLM的语义理解能力。

Result: 在英语、西班牙语和巴斯克语三种语言上评估了多种LLM，结果显示SemBench生成的排名与标准WiC数据集排名高度相关。分析表明仅需少量示例即可获得稳定且有意义的排名。

Conclusion: SemBench提供了一个轻量级、可适应且数据高效的框架，用于跨语言评估LLM的语义理解能力，解决了传统基准测试的资源密集和语言限制问题。

Abstract: Recent progress in Natural Language Processing (NLP) has been driven by the emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs), which exhibit remarkable generative and reasoning capabilities. However, despite their success, evaluating the true semantic understanding of these models remains a persistent challenge. Traditional benchmarks such as Word-in-Context (WiC) effectively probe this capability, but their creation is resource-intensive and often limited to high-resource languages. In this paper, we introduce SemBench, a framework for automatically generating synthetic benchmarks that assess the semantic competence of LLMs using only dictionary sense definitions and a sentence encoder. This approach eliminates the need for curated example sentences, making it both scalable and language-independent. We evaluate SemBench in three languages (English, Spanish, and Basque) spanning different levels of linguistic resources, and across a wide range of LLMs. Our results show that rankings derived from SemBench strongly correlate with those obtained from standard WiC datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that only a small number of examples is required to achieve stable and meaningful rankings. Overall, SemBench provides a lightweight, adaptable, and data-efficient framework for cross-lingual evaluation of semantic understanding in LLMs.

</details>


### [121] [Semi-Synthetic Parallel Data for Translation Quality Estimation: A Case Study of Dataset Building for an Under-Resourced Language Pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11743)
*Assaf Siani,Anna Kernerman,Ilan Kernerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个英希（英语-希伯来语）半合成平行数据集用于质量评估，通过创建基于典型语言模式的英语句子、使用多个机器翻译引擎翻译、基于BLEU筛选，并引入受控翻译错误来应对语言挑战，然后训练BERT和XLM-R等神经质量评估模型评估句子级机器翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对资源匮乏语言对（特别是形态句法复杂的语言）开发准确、适应性强且可靠的质量评估系统仍然是一个未解决的挑战，主要原因是平行语料库有限以及语言依赖因素多样。本研究旨在推进资源匮乏语言对（包括形态丰富的语言）的质量评估模型发展。

Method: 1. 创建半合成平行数据集：基于典型语言模式的使用示例创建英语句子，使用多个机器翻译引擎翻译成希伯来语，通过BLEU-based选择筛选输出；2. 人工评估：每个翻译片段由语言学家手动评估和评分；3. 整合专业翻译：加入自有资源中的专业翻译英希片段并分配最高质量分数；4. 引入受控翻译错误：针对性别和数一致等语言挑战；5. 模型训练：在数据集上训练BERT和XLM-R等神经质量评估模型评估句子级机器翻译质量。

Result: 研究发现数据集大小、分布平衡和错误分布对模型性能有显著影响。通过实验验证了所构建数据集的有效性，并展示了神经质量评估模型在英希语言对上的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究为资源匮乏语言对（包括形态丰富的语言）的质量评估模型发展做出了贡献，通过构建半合成数据集和训练神经模型的方法，为应对语言特定挑战提供了有效途径，并指明了未来改进质量评估性能的研究方向。

Abstract: Quality estimation (QE) plays a crucial role in machine translation (MT) workflows, as it serves to evaluate generated outputs that have no reference translations and to determine whether human post-editing or full retranslation is necessary. Yet, developing highly accurate, adaptable and reliable QE systems for under-resourced language pairs remains largely unsolved, due mainly to limited parallel corpora and to diverse language-dependent factors, such as with morphosyntactically complex languages. This study presents a semi-synthetic parallel dataset for English-to-Hebrew QE, generated by creating English sentences based on examples of usage that illustrate typical linguistic patterns, translating them to Hebrew using multiple MT engines, and filtering outputs via BLEU-based selection. Each translated segment was manually evaluated and scored by a linguist, and we also incorporated professionally translated English-Hebrew segments from our own resources, which were assigned the highest quality score. Controlled translation errors were introduced to address linguistic challenges, particularly regarding gender and number agreement, and we trained neural QE models, including BERT and XLM-R, on this dataset to assess sentence-level MT quality. Our findings highlight the impact of dataset size, distributed balance, and error distribution on model performance. We will describe the challenges, methodology and results of our experiments, and specify future directions aimed at improving QE performance. This research contributes to advancing QE models for under resourced language pairs, including morphology-rich languages.

</details>


### [122] [Compression Favors Consistency, Not Truth: When and Why Language Models Prefer Correct Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11749)
*Konstantin Krestnikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型在混合质量数据训练中偏好正确陈述的现象源于压缩一致性原则：下一个token预测倾向于支持对训练数据提供更短、更内部一致描述的假设。真理偏好仅当错误替代方案在结构上更难压缩时才出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在混合质量数据训练中为何有时偏好正确陈述，挑战传统认为模型会简单复制训练数据分布的假设，揭示底层机制。

Method: 使用小型GPT-2风格字符级transformer（3.5M-86M参数）在合成数学语料库上进行实验，控制正确和错误规则的混合比例。设置随机错误场景和连贯但数学错误的规则系统场景进行对比。在更自然语言风格的合成世界中测试效果。进行嵌入验证步骤和增加一致规则数量的额外实验。

Result: 随机错误设置中，模型强烈偏好正确补全：平衡数据下准确率83.1%，即使正确规则仅出现在10%语料中仍有67.0%准确率。用连贯但数学错误的规则系统替换随机错误后，偏好基本消失（接近随机准确率）。在更自然语言风格的合成世界中效果较弱但仍存在（57.7%）。嵌入验证步骤可在小规模下恢复对正确性的偏好，增加一致规则数量可逐步提高准确率。

Conclusion: 语言模型表现出的"真理偏好"主要是压缩压力和内部一致性偏好的副作用，而非对真理的内在驱动力。压缩一致性原则解释了为何模型在混合质量数据中仍能学习正确模式。

Abstract: Why do language models sometimes prefer correct statements even when trained on mixed-quality data? We introduce the Compression--Consistency Principle: next-token prediction favors hypotheses that allow shorter and more internally consistent descriptions of the training data. Truth bias emerges only when false alternatives are structurally harder to compress. We test this using small GPT-2-style character-level transformers (3.5M--86M parameters) on synthetic math corpora with controlled mixtures of correct and incorrect rules. In the random-error setting, models strongly prefer correct completions in paired evaluation: 83.1% accuracy at balanced data and 67.0% even when correct rules appear in only 10% of the corpus. Replacing random errors with a coherent but mathematically incorrect rule system largely eliminates the preference (near-chance accuracy). In a more natural-language-like synthetic world, the effect is weaker but still present (57.7%). Additional experiments show that embedding verification steps can restore preference for correctness even at small scale, while increasing the number of consistent rules produces a graded improvement in accuracy. Our results suggest that what appears as a "truth bias" is largely a side effect of compression pressure and preference for internal consistency, rather than an intrinsic drive toward truth. Full code and data are available at https://github.com/Rai220/compression-drives-truth.

</details>


### [123] [Legal-DC: Benchmarking Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Legal Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11772)
*Yaocong Li,Qiang Lan,Leihan Zhang,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对中文法律场景的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，构建了Legal-DC基准数据集，提出了LegRAG框架，通过法律自适应索引和双路径自反思机制提升法律条款检索的完整性和答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG技术在中文法律文档咨询应用中存在两个关键限制：1）现有基准缺乏对检索器-生成器联合评估的专业支持；2）主流RAG系统难以适应法律条款的结构化特性。需要专门的中文法律RAG评估资源和适应性框架。

Method: 1）构建Legal-DC基准数据集，包含480份法律文档和2,475个精炼问答对，每个都标注了条款级引用；2）提出LegRAG框架，集成法律自适应索引（条款边界分割）和双路径自反思机制；3）引入大语言模型自动评估方法以满足法律检索的高可靠性需求。

Result: LegRAG在关键评估指标上优于现有最先进方法1.3%至5.6%。研究提供了专门的中文法律RAG基准、实用框架和实证见解。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建Legal-DC基准数据集和提出LegRAG框架，解决了中文法律RAG系统的评估资源缺乏和结构化法律条款适应性问题，为中文法律RAG系统的发展提供了专业基准、实用框架和实证支持。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising technology for legal document consultation, yet its application in Chinese legal scenarios faces two key limitations: existing benchmarks lack specialized support for joint retriever-generator evaluation, and mainstream RAG systems often fail to accommodate the structured nature of legal provisions. To address these gaps, this study advances two core contributions: First, we constructed the Legal-DC benchmark dataset, comprising 480 legal documents (covering areas such as market regulation and contract management) and 2,475 refined question-answer pairs, each annotated with clause-level references, filling the gap for specialized evaluation resources in Chinese legal RAG. Second, we propose the LegRAG framework, which integrates legal adaptive indexing (clause-boundary segmentation) with a dual-path self-reflection mechanism to ensure clause integrity while enhancing answer accuracy. Third, we introduce automated evaluation methods for large language models to meet the high-reliability demands of legal retrieval scenarios. LegRAG outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods by 1.3% to 5.6% across key evaluation metrics. This research provides a specialized benchmark, practical framework, and empirical insights to advance the development of Chinese legal RAG systems. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/legal-dc/Legal-DC.

</details>


### [124] [Large Language Models for Biomedical Article Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11780)
*Jakub Proboszcz,Paweł Cichosz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了大型语言模型在生物医学文章分类任务中的性能，比较了不同模型、提示策略和配置，发现LLM在零样本和少样本设置下能达到与传统分类算法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是系统评估大型语言模型作为文本分类器在生物医学领域的实用性，填补现有研究中配置评估范围有限的空白，为实际应用提供指导。

Method: 研究方法包括：使用多个开源和闭源LLM模型；评估不同类型的提示策略；探索多种输出处理方法（类别预测和类别概率预测）；测试不同少样本示例数量和选择方法；在15个具有挑战性的生物医学数据集上进行评估；与传统分类算法（朴素贝叶斯、随机森林）和微调transformer模型进行性能比较。

Result: 研究结果显示：零样本提示的平均PR AUC超过0.4，少样本提示接近0.5；LLM性能接近朴素贝叶斯分类器（0.5）、随机森林算法（默认设置0.5，超参数调优0.55）和微调transformer模型（0.5）；最有效的配置包括使用输出标记概率进行类别概率预测。

Conclusion: 研究结论证实了大型语言模型在非平凡领域作为文本分类器的实用性，提供了最有效配置的实用建议，特别是使用输出标记概率进行类别概率预测的方法。

Abstract: This work presents a systematic and in-depth investigation of the utility of large language models as text classifiers for biomedical article classification. The study uses several small and mid-size open source models, as well as selected closed source ones, and is more comprehensive than most prior work with respect to the scope of evaluated configurations: different types of prompts, output processing methods for generating both class and class probability predictions, as well as few-shot example counts and selection methods. The performance of the most successful configurations is compared to that of conventional classification algorithms. The obtained average PR AUC over 15 challenging datasets above 0.4 for zero-shot prompting and nearly 0.5 for few-shot prompting comes close to that of the naïve Bayes classifier (0.5), the random forest algorithm (0.5 with default settings or 0.55 with hyperparameter tuning) and fine-tuned transformer models (0.5). These results confirm the utility of large language models as text classifiers for non-trivial domains and provide practical recommendations of the most promising setups, including in particular using output token probabilities for class probability prediction.

</details>


### [125] [Bielik-Minitron-7B: Compressing Large Language Models via Structured Pruning and Knowledge Distillation for the Polish Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11881)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Paweł Kiszczak,Sergio P. Perez,Krzysztof Ociepa,Łukasz Flis,Krzysztof Wróbel,Adrian Gwoździej*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Bielik-Minitron-7B是一个通过两阶段压缩方法从11.04B参数模型压缩到7.35B参数的欧洲语言优化模型，在保持90%基线性能的同时实现50%推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为欧洲语言创建高效的语言模型，通过压缩减少推理部署成本，同时为代表性不足的语言提供可行的模型开发路径。

Method: 采用两阶段压缩方法：1) 结构化混合剪枝（使用NVIDIA Model Optimizer）减少参数数量；2) 知识蒸馏（使用NVIDIA NeMo Framework）进行质量恢复。随后进行对齐流程：监督微调(SFT)、直接偏好优化(DPO-P)和强化学习(GRPO)。

Result: 模型参数从11.04B减少到7.35B（减少33.4%），恢复了约90%基线模型性能，推理速度提升高达50%。

Conclusion: 该方法为代表性不足的语言创建语言模型提供了高效途径，在保持原始模型质量的同时显著降低推理部署成本。

Abstract: This report details the creation of Bielik-Minitron-7B, a compressed 7.35B parameter version of the Bielik-11B-v3.0 model, specifically optimized for European languages. By leveraging a two-stage compression methodology inspired by the NVIDIA Minitron approach, we combined structured hybrid pruning and knowledge distillation to reduce the model's parameter count by 33.4%, from 11.04B to 7.35B. We utilized the NVIDIA Model Optimizer for structural pruning and the NVIDIA NeMo Framework for logit-based distillation for quality recovery. Following distillation, the model underwent a rigorous alignment pipeline consisting of Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO-P), and Reinforcement Learning (GRPO). Our final model successfully recovered approximately 90% of the baseline model's performance while providing up to 50% inference speedup. This approach demonstrates an efficient pathway to create language models for less-represented languages, preserving the original model quality while reducing inference deployment costs.

</details>


### [126] [CoMMET: To What Extent Can LLMs Perform Theory of Mind Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11915)
*Ruirui Chen,Weifeng Jiang,Chengwei Qin,Cheston Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoMMET是一个新的多模态基准数据集，用于评估大语言模型的心智理论能力，扩展了现有评估范围，涵盖更广泛的心理状态并引入多轮测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心智理论是人类社会智能的基石，随着大语言模型在现实应用中的普及，验证其社会推理能力对于有效自然的交互至关重要。现有评估基准有限，大多仅依赖文本输入且专注于信念相关任务。

Method: 提出CoMMET多模态基准数据集，受心智理论手册任务启发，涵盖更广泛的心理状态并引入多轮测试。这是首个在多轮对话设置中评估心智理论的多模态数据集。

Result: 通过对不同家族和规模的大语言模型进行全面评估，分析了当前模型的优势和局限性，并确定了未来改进方向。

Conclusion: 这项工作为理解现代大语言模型的社会认知能力提供了更深入的见解。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM)-the ability to reason about the mental states of oneself and others-is a cornerstone of human social intelligence. As Large Language Models (LLMs) become ubiquitous in real-world applications, validating their capacity for this level of social reasoning is essential for effective and natural interactions. However, existing benchmarks for assessing ToM in LLMs are limited; most rely solely on text inputs and focus narrowly on belief-related tasks. In this paper, we propose a new multimodal benchmark dataset, CoMMET, a Comprehensive Mental states and Moral Evaluation Task inspired by the Theory of Mind Booklet Task. CoMMET expands the scope of evaluation by covering a broader range of mental states and introducing multi-turn testing. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first multimodal dataset to evaluate ToM in a multi-turn conversational setting. Through a comprehensive assessment of LLMs across different families and sizes, we analyze the strengths and limitations of current models and identify directions for future improvement. Our work offers a deeper understanding of the social cognitive capabilities of modern LLMs.

</details>


### [127] [CHiL(L)Grader: Calibrated Human-in-the-Loop Short-Answer Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11957)
*Pranav Raikote,Korbinian Randl,Ioanna Miliou,Athanasios Lakes,Panagiotis Papapetrou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHiL(L)Grader是一个结合校准置信度估计与人在回路工作流的自动评分框架，通过温度缩放、选择性预测和持续学习，自动处理高置信度预测，将不确定案例路由给人工评分员，并适应不断变化的评分标准和未见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在教育评估中的扩展不仅需要准确性，还需要识别预测何时可信。指令调优模型往往过于自信，且随着课程演变其可靠性会下降，使得在高风险环境中完全自主部署不安全。需要一种能够量化不确定性并整合人工监督的自动评分系统。

Method: CHiL(L)Grader框架采用三种核心技术：1) 事后温度缩放进行置信度校准；2) 基于置信度的选择性预测，自动处理高置信度案例，将低置信度案例路由给人工评分员；3) 持续学习，从教师反馈中学习以增强评分能力并适应新评分标准和未见问题。

Result: 在三个短答案评分数据集上，CHiL(L)Grader能够自动评分35-65%的答案，达到专家级质量(QWK >= 0.80)。接受与拒绝预测之间的QWK差距为0.347，证实了基于置信度路由的有效性。每个纠正周期都通过从教师反馈中学习来增强模型的评分能力。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化是可靠AI辅助评分的关键。CHiL(L)Grader通过结合校准置信度估计与人在回路工作流，实现了安全、自适应且高效的自动评分系统，能够在高风险教育环境中可靠部署。

Abstract: Scaling educational assessment with large language models requires not just accuracy, but the ability to recognize when predictions are trustworthy. Instruction-tuned models tend to be overconfident, and their reliability deteriorates as curricula evolve, making fully autonomous deployment unsafe in high-stakes settings. We introduce CHiL(L)Grader, the first automated grading framework that incorporates calibrated confidence estimation into a human-in-the-loop workflow. Using post-hoc temperature scaling, confidence-based selective prediction, and continual learning, CHiL(L)Grader automates only high-confidence predictions while routing uncertain cases to human graders, and adapts to evolving rubrics and unseen questions. Across three short-answer grading datasets, CHiL(L)Grader automatically scores 35-65% of responses at expert-level quality (QWK >= 0.80). A QWK gap of 0.347 between accepted and rejected predictions confirms the effectiveness of the confidence-based routing. Each correction cycle strengthens the model's grading capability as it learns from teacher feedback. These results show that uncertainty quantification is key for reliable AI-assisted grading.

</details>


### [128] [Just Use XML: Revisiting Joint Translation and Label Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12021)
*Thennal D K,Chris Biemann,Hans Ole Hatzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LabelPigeon框架通过XML标签联合执行翻译和标签投影，在11种语言中提升翻译质量，在27种语言和3个下游任务中显著改善跨语言迁移效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签投影方法通常在机器翻译后作为独立步骤执行，而先前结合两者的工作报告翻译质量下降。本文重新评估这一主张，探索联合翻译和标签投影的可能性

Method: 提出LabelPigeon框架，通过XML标签联合执行翻译和标签投影。设计直接评估方案，在11种语言中测试，并在203种语言和不同注释复杂度下评估翻译质量

Result: LabelPigeon优于基线方法，在11种语言中积极提升翻译质量。在203种语言和不同注释复杂度下观察到一致的改进。在27种语言和3个下游任务中，跨语言迁移效果显著提升，NER任务上最高提升+39.9 F1

Conclusion: XML标签的标签投影提供有效且高效的标签迁移，同时不损害翻译质量。联合方法优于分离方法，为跨语言迁移提供了新的有效途径

Abstract: Label projection is an effective technique for cross-lingual transfer, extending span-annotated datasets from a high-resource language to low-resource ones. Most approaches perform label projection as a separate step after machine translation, and prior work that combines the two reports degraded translation quality. We re-evaluate this claim with LabelPigeon, a novel framework that jointly performs translation and label projection via XML tags. We design a direct evaluation scheme for label projection, and find that LabelPigeon outperforms baselines and actively improves translation quality in 11 languages. We further assess translation quality across 203 languages and varying annotation complexity, finding consistent improvement attributed to additional fine-tuning. Finally, across 27 languages and three downstream tasks, we report substantial gains in cross-lingual transfer over comparable work, up to +39.9 F1 on NER. Overall, our results demonstrate that XML-tagged label projection provides effective and efficient label transfer without compromising translation quality.

</details>


### [129] [Translationese as a Rational Response to Translation Task Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12050)
*Maria Kunilovskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 翻译文本与目标语言原生文本存在系统性差异（翻译腔），本研究提出翻译腔源于翻译任务本身的认知负荷，并通过信息论指标量化翻译难度来预测翻译腔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻译腔现象已有多种解释（如干扰、简化、社会文化因素等），但缺乏统一的理论框架。本研究旨在验证翻译腔是否源于翻译任务本身的认知负荷，为翻译腔提供认知解释。

Method: 使用双向英德语料库（书面和口语子库），将翻译腔操作化为自动分类器生成的片段级翻译度分数。翻译难度概念化为源文本复杂度和跨语言迁移难度，主要通过基于LLM惊奇值的信息论指标量化，辅以传统句法和语义特征。

Result: 翻译腔可部分由翻译任务难度解释，尤其在英译德方向更明显。跨语言迁移难度比源文本复杂度贡献更大。信息论指标在书面模式中与传统特征相当或更优，但在口语模式中无优势。源文本句法复杂度和翻译方案熵是跨语言对和模式中最强的翻译腔预测因子。

Conclusion: 翻译腔确实与翻译任务难度相关，支持认知负荷解释。信息论指标在书面翻译中有应用价值，但口语翻译可能需要不同特征。研究为翻译腔提供了基于认知任务的统一解释框架。

Abstract: Translations systematically diverge from texts originally produced in the target language, a phenomenon widely referred to as translationese. Translationese has been attributed to production tendencies (e.g. interference, simplification), socio-cultural variables, and language-pair effects, yet a unified explanatory account is still lacking. We propose that translationese reflects cognitive load inherent in the translation task itself. We test whether observable translationese can be predicted from quantifiable measures of translation task difficulty. Translationese is operationalised as a segment-level translatedness score produced by an automatic classifier. Translation task difficulty is conceptualised as comprising source-text and cross-lingual transfer components, operationalised mainly through information-theoretic metrics based on LLM surprisal, complemented by established syntactic and semantic alternatives. We use a bidirectional English-German corpus comprising written and spoken subcorpora. Results indicate that translationese can be partly explained by translation task difficulty, especially in English-to-German. For most experiments, cross-lingual transfer difficulty contributes more than source-text complexity. Information-theoretic indicators match or outperform traditional features in written mode, but offer no advantage in spoken mode. Source-text syntactic complexity and translation-solution entropy emerged as the strongest predictors of translationese across language pairs and modes.

</details>


### [130] [To Words and Beyond: Probing Large Language Models for Sentence-Level Psycholinguistic Norms of Memorability and Reading Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12105)
*Thomas Hikaru Clark,Carlos Arriaga,Javier Conde,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs通过微调可以预测句子记忆性和阅读时间等句子级特征，与人类判断相关，但零样本/少样本表现不佳，表明需要谨慎使用LLM提示作为人类认知测量的替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLMs可以预测词汇级心理语言学特征（如效价、唤醒度），但对于句子级特征（如句子记忆性和阅读时间）尚未研究。这些特征涉及词与词在句子上下文中的关系，需要探索LLMs是否也能有效预测这些更复杂的认知特征。

Method: 扩展先前方法，研究LLMs对句子记忆性和阅读时间的预测能力。采用两种方式：1）零样本/少样本提示（类似人类研究中的问题）；2）监督微调。将LLMs预测结果与人类判断的基准值进行比较，并与可解释基线预测器对比。

Result: 通过微调，LLMs能够提供与人类判断相关的句子记忆性和阅读时间估计，其预测能力超过可解释基线预测器。然而，零样本和少样本表现非常不一致，表明LLMs包含有用的句子级特征信息，但需要监督训练才能有效提取。

Conclusion: LLMs确实包含关于句子级认知特征的有用信息，但需要监督微调才能有效提取。零样本/少样本提示作为人类认知测量替代的可靠性有限，使用时需要谨慎。研究为LLMs在心理语言学应用提供了新证据，同时强调了方法选择的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been shown to produce estimates of psycholinguistic norms, such as valence, arousal, or concreteness, for words and multiword expressions, that correlate with human judgments. These estimates are obtained by prompting an LLM, in zero-shot fashion, with a question similar to those used in human studies. Meanwhile, for other norms such as lexical decision time or age of acquisition, LLMs require supervised fine-tuning to obtain results that align with ground-truth values. In this paper, we extend this approach to the previously unstudied features of sentence memorability and reading times, which involve the relationship between multiple words in a sentence-level context. Our results show that via fine-tuning, models can provide estimates that correlate with human-derived norms and exceed the predictive power of interpretable baseline predictors, demonstrating that LLMs contain useful information about sentence-level features. At the same time, our results show very mixed zero-shot and few-shot performance, providing further evidence that care is needed when using LLM-prompting as a proxy for human cognitive measures.

</details>


### [131] [SommBench: Assessing Sommelier Expertise of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12117)
*William Brach,Tomas Bedej,Jacob Nielsen,Jacob Pichna,Juraj Bedej,Eemeli Saarensilta,Julie Dupouy,Gianluca Barmina,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Kristian Košťál,Michal Ries,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SommBench是一个多语言基准测试，用于评估语言模型的侍酒师专业知识，该领域深度依赖于嗅觉和味觉感官。基准包含三个任务：葡萄酒理论问答、葡萄酒特征补全和食物-葡萄酒搭配，涵盖8种语言。结果表明，语言模型在理论知识上表现良好，但在需要感官判断的任务上仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的快速发展，系统评估其多语言和多文化能力变得日益重要。以往的文化评估基准主要关注可以编码为语言形式的基本文化知识。本研究提出SommBench，旨在测试语言模型是否能够通过文本描述来模拟专家级的感官判断能力，特别是侍酒师这一深度依赖嗅觉和味觉的领域。

Method: SommBench包含三个主要任务：葡萄酒理论问答（WTQA）、葡萄酒特征补全（WFC）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（FWP）。基准涵盖8种语言（英语、斯洛伐克语、瑞典语、芬兰语、德语、丹麦语、意大利语、西班牙语），以区分语言模型的葡萄酒专业知识与其语言技能。数据集通过与专业侍酒师和各语言母语者密切合作开发，包含1,024个理论问答问题、1,000个特征补全示例和1,000个食物-葡萄酒搭配示例。

Result: 评估了包括Gemini 2.5（闭源权重）和GPT-OSS、Qwen 3（开源权重）在内的流行语言模型。结果显示：最强大的模型在葡萄酒理论问答上表现良好（闭源模型最高达97%正确率），但特征补全（峰值65%）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（MCC在0到0.39之间）更具挑战性。这表明语言模型在需要感官判断的任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: SommBench作为一个有趣且具有挑战性的基准，能够有效评估语言模型的侍酒师专业知识。研究结果表明，虽然语言模型通过文本学习能够掌握理论知识，但在需要模拟感官判断的任务上仍面临困难。该基准为评估语言模型在多感官领域的理解能力提供了新的测试平台。

Abstract: With the rapid advances of large language models, it becomes increasingly important to systematically evaluate their multilingual and multicultural capabilities. Previous cultural evaluation benchmarks focus mainly on basic cultural knowledge that can be encoded in linguistic form. Here, we propose SommBench, a multilingual benchmark to assess sommelier expertise, a domain deeply grounded in the senses of smell and taste. While language models learn about sensory properties exclusively through textual descriptions, SommBench tests whether this textual grounding is sufficient to emulate expert-level sensory judgment. SommBench comprises three main tasks: Wine Theory Question Answering (WTQA), Wine Feature Completion (WFC), and Food-Wine Pairing (FWP). SommBench is available in multiple languages: English, Slovak, Swedish, Finnish, German, Danish, Italian, and Spanish. This helps separate a language model's wine expertise from its language skills. The benchmark datasets were developed in close collaboration with a professional sommelier and native speakers of the respective languages, resulting in 1,024 wine theory question-answering questions, 1,000 wine feature-completion examples, and 1,000 food-wine pairing examples. We provide results for the most popular language models, including closed-weights models such as Gemini 2.5, and open-weights models, such as GPT-OSS and Qwen 3. Our results show that the most capable models perform well on wine theory question answering (up to 97% correct with a closed-weights model), yet feature completion (peaking at 65%) and food-wine pairing show (MCC ranging between 0 and 0.39) turn out to be more challenging. These results position SommBench as an interesting and challenging benchmark for evaluating the sommelier expertise of language models. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/sommify/sommbench.

</details>


### [132] [LifeSim: Long-Horizon User Life Simulator for Personalized Assistant Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12152)
*Feiyu Duan,Xuanjing Huang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LifeSim-Eval：基于BDI认知模型的用户模拟器与多场景长时域个性化助手评估基准，揭示当前LLM在隐式意图理解和长期偏好建模方面的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化助手基准与现实用户-助手交互存在偏差，未能充分捕捉外部环境和用户认知状态的复杂性，需要更贴近真实交互的评估框架

Method: 提出LifeSim用户模拟器，采用信念-愿望-意图（BDI）认知模型在物理环境中生成连贯的生活轨迹，模拟意图驱动的用户交互行为；基于此构建LifeSim-Eval基准，涵盖8个生活领域、1200个多样化场景，采用多轮交互方式评估模型能力

Result: 实验表明，当前大语言模型在单场景和长时域设置下，处理隐式意图和长期用户偏好建模方面存在显著局限性

Conclusion: LifeSim-Eval为个性化助手评估提供了更贴近现实的基准，揭示了当前LLM在理解用户复杂认知状态方面的不足，为未来研究指明了改进方向

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has accelerated progress toward universal AI assistants. However, existing benchmarks for personalized assistants remain misaligned with real-world user-assistant interactions, failing to capture the complexity of external contexts and users' cognitive states. To bridge this gap, we propose LifeSim, a user simulator that models user cognition through the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) model within physical environments for coherent life trajectories generation, and simulates intention-driven user interactive behaviors. Based on LifeSim, we introduce LifeSim-Eval, a comprehensive benchmark for multi-scenario, long-horizon personalized assistance. LifeSim-Eval covers 8 life domains and 1,200 diverse scenarios, and adopts a multi-turn interactive method to assess models' abilities to complete explicit and implicit intentions, recover user profiles, and produce high-quality responses. Under both single-scenario and long-horizon settings, our experiments reveal that current LLMs face significant limitations in handling implicit intention and long-term user preference modeling.

</details>


### [133] [QAQ: Bidirectional Semantic Coherence for Selecting High-Quality Synthetic Code Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12165)
*Jiayin Lei,Ming Ma,Yunxi Duan,Chenxi Li,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QAQ框架，通过反向互信息(RMI)评估合成代码生成数据的质量，基于答案预测查询的能力(Q|A)而非传统IFD方法(A|Q)，有效区分任务复杂性与模型幻觉，仅用25%数据达到全数据训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前合成数据训练代码生成模型存在显著噪声和幻觉问题，现有数据选择方法如指令跟随难度(IFD)在评估A|Q时存在模糊性，无法区分内在任务复杂性和模型生成的幻觉，需要更有效的质量评估方法。

Method: 提出QAQ框架，定义反向互信息(RMI)量化答案对查询的信息增益(Q|A)。分析RMI极端值：低RMI表示语义错位，过高RMI可能包含LLM易识别的缺陷模式。引入强弱模型分歧策略识别有效但具挑战性的样本。

Result: 在WarriorCoder数据集上实验表明，使用分层RMI仅选择25%数据即可达到全数据训练可比性能，显著优于现有数据选择方法，降低计算成本同时保持模型能力。

Conclusion: QAQ框架强调合成数据整理中双向语义一致性的重要性，通过反向评估(Q|A)提供可扩展路径，有效减少计算成本而不牺牲模型性能，为合成数据质量评估提供新视角。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become essential for training code generation models, yet it introduces significant noise and hallucinations that are difficult to detect with current metrics. Existing data selection methods like Instruction-Following Difficulty (IFD) typically assess how hard a model generates an answer given a query ($A|Q$). However, this metric is ambiguous on noisy synthetic data, where low probability can distinguish between intrinsic task complexity and model-generated hallucinations. Here, we propose QAQ, a novel data selection framework that evaluates data quality from the reverse direction: how well can the answer predict the query ($Q|A$)? We define Reverse Mutual Information (RMI) to quantify the information gain about the query conditioned on the answer. Our analyses reveal that both extremes of RMI signal quality issues: low RMI indicates semantic misalignment, while excessively high RMI may contain defect patterns that LLMs easily recognize. Furthermore, we introduce a selection strategy based on the disagreement between strong and weak models to identify samples that are valid yet challenging. Experiments on the WarriorCoder dataset demonstrate that selecting just 25% of data using stratified RMI achieves comparable performance to full-data training, significantly outperforming existing data selection methods. Our approach highlights the importance of bidirectional semantic coherence in synthetic data curation, offering a scalable pathway to reduce computational costs without sacrificing model capability.

</details>


### [134] [Long-Context Encoder Models for Polish Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12191)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Marek Kozłowski,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz,Paweł Klimiuk,Przemysław Boruta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个支持8192 tokens长文本处理的波兰语编码器模型，通过两阶段训练和知识蒸馏获得压缩变体，在25个任务上超越现有波兰语和多语言模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管解码器架构的LLMs主导NLP领域，但编码器架构在判别任务上仍具有成本效益和参数效率优势。然而经典编码器如BERT受限于短上下文窗口，无法处理长文档，特别是针对波兰语缺乏高质量的长文本处理模型

Method: 采用两阶段训练：1) 位置嵌入适配；2) 全参数连续预训练。同时通过知识蒸馏训练压缩模型变体。模型支持8192 tokens的长序列处理

Result: 在25个任务上评估，包括KLEJ基准、新引入的金融任务套件(FinBench)以及其他分类回归任务。模型在波兰语和多语言模型中取得最佳平均性能，在长上下文任务上显著超越竞争方案，同时在短文本上保持可比质量

Conclusion: 成功开发了高质量波兰语长文本编码器模型，解决了经典编码器上下文窗口限制问题，为波兰语NLP提供了有效的长文档处理解决方案

Abstract: While decoder-only Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently dominated the NLP landscape, encoder-only architectures remain a cost-effective and parameter-efficient standard for discriminative tasks. However, classic encoders like BERT are limited by a short context window, which is insufficient for processing long documents. In this paper, we address this limitation for the Polish by introducing a high-quality Polish model capable of processing sequences of up to 8192 tokens. The model was developed by employing a two-stage training procedure that involves positional embedding adaptation and full parameter continuous pre-training. Furthermore, we propose compressed model variants trained via knowledge distillation. The models were evaluated on 25 tasks, including the KLEJ benchmark, a newly introduced financial task suite (FinBench), and other classification and regression tasks, specifically those requiring long-document understanding. The results demonstrate that our model achieves the best average performance among Polish and multilingual models, significantly outperforming competitive solutions in long-context tasks while maintaining comparable quality on short texts.

</details>


### [135] [Sparking Scientific Creativity via LLM-Driven Interdisciplinary Inspiration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12226)
*Priyanka Kargupta,Shuhaib Mehri,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Idea-Catalyst是一个新颖的框架，通过系统识别跨学科见解来支持人类和大型语言模型的创造性推理，避免过早锚定具体解决方案，提高研究的新颖性和洞察力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管跨学科研究能带来更大、更长期的影响，但大多数工作仍局限于单一学科领域。现有的AI辅助科学发现方法多侧重于快速设计实验和解决方案，而忽略了驱动创造性跨学科突破的探索性、协作性推理过程。因此，需要开发能够增强而非自动化科学发现推理过程的工具。

Method: Idea-Catalyst框架包含三个关键元认知特征：1)定义和评估研究目标；2)了解领域的机遇和未解决挑战；3)基于潜在影响的跨学科思想战略探索。具体方法包括：将抽象目标分解为核心研究问题，分析领域进展和开放挑战，将这些挑战重新表述为领域无关的概念问题，从外部学科检索类似问题的解决方案，然后将这些见解重新语境化到目标领域，并按跨学科潜力对源领域进行排名。

Result: 实证研究表明，这种有针对性的整合方法将平均新颖性提高了21%，洞察力提高了16%，同时保持对原始研究问题的针对性。

Conclusion: Idea-Catalyst框架能够有效支持跨学科创造性推理，通过系统化的方法识别和整合外部学科的见解，显著提高研究的新颖性和洞察力，为科学发现提供了增强而非自动化的支持工具。

Abstract: Despite interdisciplinary research leading to larger and longer-term impact, most work remains confined to single-domain academic silos. Recent AI-based approaches to scientific discovery show promise for interdisciplinary research, but many prioritize rapidly designing experiments and solutions, bypassing the exploratory, collaborative reasoning processes that drive creative interdisciplinary breakthroughs. As a result, prior efforts largely prioritize automating scientific discovery rather than augmenting the reasoning processes that underlie scientific disruption. We present Idea-Catalyst, a novel framework that systematically identifies interdisciplinary insights to support creative reasoning in both humans and large language models. Starting from an abstract research goal, Idea-Catalyst is designed to assist the brainstorming stage, explicitly avoiding premature anchoring on specific solutions. The framework embodies key metacognitive features of interdisciplinary reasoning: (a) defining and assessing research goals, (b) awareness of a domain's opportunities and unresolved challenges, and (c) strategic exploration of interdisciplinary ideas based on impact potential. Concretely, Idea-Catalyst decomposes an abstract goal (e.g., improving human-AI collaboration) into core target-domain research questions that guide the analysis of progress and open challenges within that domain. These challenges are reformulated as domain-agnostic conceptual problems, enabling retrieval from external disciplines (e.g., Psychology, Sociology) that address analogous issues. By synthesizing and recontextualizing insights from these domains back into the target domain, Idea-Catalyst ranks source domains by their interdisciplinary potential. Empirically, this targeted integration improves average novelty by 21% and insightfulness by 16%, while remaining grounded in the original research problem.

</details>


### [136] [SciMDR: Benchmarking and Advancing Scientific Multimodal Document Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12249)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Chengye Wang,Rilyn Han,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciMDR：通过合成与再锚定框架构建的大规模科学多模态文档推理数据集，包含30万QA对和专家标注的评估基准，显著提升模型在科学文档级推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建科学多模态文档推理数据集面临规模、忠实性和现实性之间的固有权衡，需要一种能够平衡这些因素的框架来支持基础模型训练。

Method: 提出合成与再锚定两阶段框架：1) 以主张为中心的QA合成，生成忠实、独立的QA对和推理链；2) 文档级再锚定，将QA对程序化地重新嵌入完整文档任务以确保现实复杂性。

Result: 构建了SciMDR数据集（30万QA对，覆盖2万篇科学论文）和SciMDR-Eval专家标注评估基准。实验表明，在SciMDR上微调的模型在多个科学QA基准测试中，特别是需要复杂文档级推理的任务上，取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 合成与再锚定框架有效解决了科学多模态数据集构建中的权衡问题，SciMDR数据集能够显著提升模型在科学文档理解任务上的性能，为科学多模态推理提供了有价值的训练资源。

Abstract: Constructing scientific multimodal document reasoning datasets for foundation model training involves an inherent trade-off among scale, faithfulness, and realism. To address this challenge, we introduce the synthesize-and-reground framework, a two-stage pipeline comprising: (1) Claim-Centric QA Synthesis, which generates faithful, isolated QA pairs and reasoning on focused segments, and (2) Document-Scale Regrounding, which programmatically re-embeds these pairs into full-document tasks to ensure realistic complexity. Using this framework, we construct SciMDR, a large-scale training dataset for cross-modal comprehension, comprising 300K QA pairs with explicit reasoning chains across 20K scientific papers. We further construct SciMDR-Eval, an expert-annotated benchmark to evaluate multimodal comprehension within full-length scientific workflows. Experiments demonstrate that models fine-tuned on SciMDR achieve significant improvements across multiple scientific QA benchmarks, particularly in those tasks requiring complex document-level reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV是一种用于移动机器人的遮挡感知最优视点规划方法，专门针对被遮挡人体的观测优化，在仿真和真实实验中成功率超过90%，显著提升观测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜索、分类、灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性经常降低下游感知性能。现有最优视点方法主要优化通用探索或长时程覆盖，未针对在真实运动约束下获取被遮挡人员的单个可用观测这一即时目标。

Method: OA-NBV集成了感知和运动规划，通过目标中心可见性模型对候选视点进行评分，该模型考虑了遮挡、目标尺度和目标完整性，同时将候选视点限制在可行的机器人姿态。

Result: OA-NBV在仿真和真实世界实验中均达到超过90%的成功率，而基线NBV方法在遮挡情况下性能急剧下降。相比最强基线，OA-NBV将归一化目标面积至少提高81%，关键点可见性至少提高58%。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一个即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化的人类中心下游任务，能够显著改善对被遮挡人员的观测质量。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [138] [DRAFTO: Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11074)
*Yichang Feng,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAFTO是一种新的轨迹优化算法，通过解耦降维空间优化和自适应可行性修复，结合高斯-牛顿下降和二次规划，高效处理关节限制和约束优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法在重复约束优化和关节限制可行性处理方面效率不高，需要一种能减少重复优化次数、有效处理关节限制并确保全局收敛性的新算法。

Method: 1. 构建包含平滑性、安全性、关节限制和任务要求的约束目标函数；2. 将轨迹参数化为基函数系数的优化问题；3. 解耦为降维空间高斯-牛顿下降（主迭代）和约束二次规划（初始化和终端可行性修复）；4. 采用带非单调策略的两阶段接受规则，使用铰链平方惩罚处理不等式约束以确保全局收敛性。

Result: 在基准测试中，DRAFTO相比CHOMP、TrajOpt、GPMP2、FACTO等优化型规划器以及RRT-Connect、RRT*、PRM等采样型规划器，展现出更高的效率和可靠性；从抽屉抓取物体的实验进一步证明了其在复杂操作任务中的实施潜力。

Conclusion: DRAFTO通过解耦优化策略有效减少了重复约束优化次数，成功处理了关节限制可行性问题，在多样场景和任务中表现出高效可靠的性能，适用于复杂操作任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new algorithm for trajectory optimization, Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization (DRAFTO). It first constructs a constrained objective that accounts for smoothness, safety, joint limits, and task requirements. Then, it optimizes the coefficients, which are the coordinates of a set of basis functions for trajectory parameterization. To reduce the number of repeated constrained optimizations while handling joint-limit feasibility, the optimization is decoupled into a reduced-space Gauss-Newton (GN) descent for the main iterations and constrained quadratic programming for initialization and terminal feasibility repair. The two-phase acceptance rule with a non-monotone policy is applied to the GN model, which uses a hinge-squared penalty for inequality constraints, to ensure globalizability. The results of our benchmark tests against optimization-based planners, such as CHOMP, TrajOpt, GPMP2, and FACTO, and sampling-based planners, such as RRT-Connect, RRT*, and PRM, validate the high efficiency and reliability across diverse scenarios and tasks. The experiment involving grabbing an object from a drawer further demonstrates the potential for implementation in complex manipulation tasks. The supplemental video is available at https://youtu.be/XisFI37YyTQ.

</details>


### [139] [SELF-VLA: A Skill Enhanced Agentic Vision-Language-Action Framework for Contact-Rich Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11080)
*Chang Liu,Sibo Tian,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SELF-VLA是一个集成显式拆卸技能的智能视觉-语言-动作框架，显著优于现有端到端VLA模型，在接触密集型拆卸任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人拆卸系统存在以下问题：1) 每个子任务需要大量数据准备、模型训练和系统管理；2) 方法通常是任务和组件特定的，难以处理EoL产品的变异性；3) 现有VLA模型在标准机器人操作任务上表现良好，但在复杂、接触密集、长时程的工业拆卸任务中应用有限。

Method: 提出SELF-VLA框架，该框架集成显式拆卸技能，通过智能VLA模型处理复杂、接触密集的拆卸任务，实现顺序精确操作。

Result: 实验研究表明，该框架在两个接触密集型拆卸任务上显著优于当前最先进的端到端VLA模型。

Conclusion: SELF-VLA框架通过集成显式拆卸技能，有效解决了当前机器人拆卸系统的局限性，提高了在复杂工业拆卸任务中的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Disassembly automation has long been pursued to address the growing demand for efficient and proper recovery of valuable components from the end-of-life (EoL) electronic products. Existing approaches have demonstrated promising and regimented performance by decomposing the disassembly process into different subtasks. However, each subtask typically requires extensive data preparation, model training, and system management. Moreover, these approaches are often task- and component-specific, making them poorly suited to handle the variability and uncertainty of EoL products and limiting their generalization capabilities. All these factors restrict the practical deployment of current robotic disassembly systems and leave them highly reliant on human labor. With the recent development of foundation models in robotics, vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive performance on standard robotic manipulation tasks, but their applicability to complex, contact-rich, and long-horizon industrial practices like disassembly, which requires sequential and precise manipulation, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose SELF-VLA, an agentic VLA framework that integrates explicit disassembly skills. Experimental studies demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art end-to-end VLA models on two contact-rich disassembly tasks. The video illustration can be found via https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/IROS-VLA-Video.mp4.

</details>


### [140] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，通过金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，实现有限带宽下的实时协作SLAM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决云边协同多机器人SLAM中带宽限制和长通信链路导致的性能下降问题，克服终端设备计算、通信和存储能力有限以及单机器人执行效率低的问题

Method: 1) 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，降低特征跟踪计算成本；2) 设计机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3) 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述子，并进行无损编码压缩

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上验证：相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，能实现更低数据量的特征传输；相比先进的集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下能达到相同或更好的定位精度

Conclusion: 提出的分层架构和轻量化SLAM方法有效解决了云边协同多机器人SLAM的带宽限制问题，实现了有限带宽下的实时远程信息传输，同时保持了SLAM精度

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [141] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM将控制变量从绝对动作重构为残差动作（相对于上一步的增量调整），通过观测差分编码器显式建模相邻帧变化，在Dreamer式潜在动力学模型中实现更稳定、高效的规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的RL框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务相关的、先验未知的，通常会导致振荡或低效控制。需要一种更稳定、更适合机器人连续控制的方法。

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型（ResWM），将控制变量从绝对动作重构为残差动作（相对于上一步的增量调整）。引入观测差分编码器显式建模相邻帧变化，生成紧凑的潜在动力学表示。在Dreamer式潜在动力学模型中集成ResWM，无需额外超参数，在残差动作空间中进行想象展开和策略优化。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上的实验表明，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面实现了一致性改进，显著超越了Dreamer和TD-MPC等强基线。产生更稳定、能量效率更高的动作轨迹，这对真实世界机器人系统部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接RL算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了一个简单而强大的原则，能够实现更平滑的探索、更低的控制方差和更可靠的规划。

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [142] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于敏捷固定翼无人机的鲁棒协同设计框架，将参数不确定性和风扰动直接集成到并发优化过程中，通过双层方法优化物理设计并评估性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计框架往往忽视自主系统在非结构化真实环境中所需的鲁棒性，导致敏捷无人机在飞行包线边缘运行时对扰动和模型失配敏感

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集合评估性能

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，该策略始终优于确定性基线方法，结果表明鲁棒协同设计策略能够定制气动特征（如机翼位置和展弦比），在任务性能和扰动抑制之间实现最优权衡

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒协同设计框架能够有效解决敏捷固定翼无人机在不确定环境中的设计问题，通过集成参数不确定性和风扰动，实现了更稳健的系统设计

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [143] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的数值求解器，用于非凸环境中连续轨迹计算，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图实现联合优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非凸环境中存在搜索空间受限和需要复杂热启动的问题，需要一种能同时优化离散空间和连续时间域、且对初始化鲁棒的求解器

Method: 定制化ADMM实现，包含两个关键组件：1) 多项式参数化轨迹，使原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划的时空分配图，将松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索

Result: 相比现有方法，该方法能访问更大的搜索空间，发现更优轨迹，且结构鲁棒性确保从简单初始化可靠收敛，消除了复杂热启动的瓶颈

Conclusion: 提出的求解器在非凸环境轨迹规划中具有显著优势，通过联合优化离散空间和连续时间域，实现了更好的轨迹质量和收敛可靠性

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [144] [Novelty Adaptation Through Hybrid Large Language Model (LLM)-Symbolic Planning and LLM-guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11351)
*Hong Lu,Pierrick Lorang,Timothy R. Duggan,Jivko Sinapov,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号架构，结合符号规划、强化学习和大型语言模型来处理动态开放世界中的新颖对象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态开放世界环境中，自主智能体常遇到新颖对象，传统符号规划器因缺乏相应操作符而无法生成有效计划，需要解决这一局限性

Method: 采用神经符号架构，利用LLM的常识推理能力识别缺失操作符，符号AI规划器生成计划，LLM编写奖励函数指导强化学习智能体学习新操作符的控制策略

Result: 在操作符发现和连续机器人领域操作符学习方面优于现有最先进方法

Conclusion: 提出的神经符号架构能有效处理动态开放世界中的新颖对象，通过整合符号规划、强化学习和LLM实现更好的操作符发现和学习性能

Abstract: In dynamic open-world environments, autonomous agents often encounter novelties that hinder their ability to find plans to achieve their goals. Specifically, traditional symbolic planners fail to generate plans when the robot's planning domain lacks the operators that enable it to interact appropriately with novel objects in the environment. We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that integrates symbolic planning, reinforcement learning, and a large language model (LLM) to learn how to handle novel objects. In particular, we leverage the common sense reasoning capability of the LLM to identify missing operators, generate plans with the symbolic AI planner, and write reward functions to guide the reinforcement learning agent in learning control policies for newly identified operators. Our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in operator discovery as well as operator learning in continuous robotic domains.

</details>


### [145] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDrift攻击利用平面镜反射在LiDAR扫描中产生鬼点，系统性偏差扫描匹配对应关系，成功攻击具有先进干扰缓解机制的生产级安全LiDAR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击依赖信号注入，需要传感器特定时序知识且易被现代防御机制（如时序混淆和注入拒绝）缓解。本研究探索无需注入的替代攻击方法，利用镜面反射原理。

Method: 提出MirrorDrift攻击，使用可驱动的平面镜产生LiDAR扫描中的鬼点，系统性地偏差扫描匹配对应关系。优化镜面放置、对准和驱动参数。

Result: 仿真中使平均姿态误差增加6.1倍，三个SLAM系统平均APE达2.29-3.31米。真实世界实验中，在现代LiDAR上诱导高达6.03米的定位误差，首次成功攻击生产级安全LiDAR。

Conclusion: 镜面反射提供了一种无需信号注入的LiDAR SLAM攻击方法，能有效绕过现代防御机制，揭示了基于几何一致性的扫描匹配假设的脆弱性。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [146] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-SLAMSpoof是一种针对LiDAR SLAM的新型攻击方法，即使在特征丰富的环境中也能通过激光干扰注入虚假测量，同时提出了基于惯性航位推算的ISD-SLAM防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗攻击在特征丰富的真实环境（如城市和室内空间）中成功率较低，需要开发更有效的攻击方法来暴露LiDAR SLAM的固有漏洞，并建立实用的防御机制。

Method: 攻击方面：设计空间注入形状和时间协调的动态注入模式，基于扫描匹配原理指导攻击；防御方面：提出ISD-SLAM，仅利用自主系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号进行检测。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在真实特征丰富环境中显著提高了攻击成功率，ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LiDAR SLAM的固有安全漏洞，提出了首个仅使用标准车载传感器的实用防御方案，为提升自主系统的安全性和可靠性提供了关键见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [147] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手部影射与重定向管道，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、坐标变换和逆运动学求解，实现低成本机械臂的遥操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临的主要挑战是将复杂的手部关节运动映射到机器人关节指令。现有方法需要复杂的传感器或标记，限制了实际应用。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机采集数据；通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点；利用深度感知进行3D反投影；将坐标转换到机器人坐标系；在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘法求解逆运动学；设计四层回退机制的夹爪控制器；通过LeRobot框架在物理模拟中预览后执行。

Result: 在结构化拾放任务（5格网格，每格10次抓取）中达到90%成功率；与四种视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）相比表现良好；在非结构化真实环境（超市、药店）中成功率降至9.3%（N=75），主要受物体遮挡影响。

Conclusion: 无标记分析重定向方法在结构化环境中表现良好，但在非结构化环境中因手部遮挡而受限，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [148] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该博士论文研究了如何通过部署时机制提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性，包括运行时监控、数据驱动的策略可解释性框架以及可靠的长时程任务执行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型机器人策略在实际部署中存在可靠性问题，包括分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖等因素会削弱系统性能，需要部署时机制来提升策略的可靠性。

Method: 提出了三类互补的部署时机制：1) 运行时监控方法，通过检测闭环策略行为不一致性和任务进度偏差来预测即将发生的失败；2) 基于影响函数的数据中心化策略可解释性框架，将部署时的成功与失败追溯到有影响的训练演示；3) 可靠长时程任务执行方法，将策略协调问题形式化为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率，并扩展到语言指定任务的可行性感知任务规划。

Result: 这些机制共同为学习型机器人策略的可靠、实际使用提供了实用基础，能够在不依赖失败数据或任务特定监督的情况下提升部署时的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过专注于部署的核心挑战，这些贡献推进了学习型机器人策略在实际应用中可靠使用的基础，未来的持续进展对于实现可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [149] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略集成到手术器械跟踪流程中，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，实现高效准确的手术器械位姿和关节配置联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而基于渲染的方法则面临计算成本高和收敛性差的问题。
